The user wants a "turn-based platformer game" — an unusual combination. A platformer where you move tile by tile (like a grid-based roguelike, or puzzle games like *Super Hexagon*… no, more like *Enter the Gungeon*'s turn system, or better: a grid-based action platformer that resolves in turns). Let me design something distinctive.

Concept: "TURN JUMP" / "STEP BY STEP" — a turn-based platformer where each of your actions (move left/right, jump) consumes 1 turn. After you act, enemies move in their own turn. This is like *The Binding of Isaac* meets a platformer, or actually closer to classic grid games. Or: an action-platformer feel but with discrete steps — press arrow key and character animates smoothly, then the world responds (enemies step, spikes activate, platforms crumble).

Let me design a solid game:

**Title ideas:** "STEPWRIGHT" / "TURNABOUT" / "GRIDLEAP" / "ONE STEP AT A TIME". I like "STEPWRIGHT" — evokes a craftsman of steps. Or "TURN & LEAP". Let's go with "STEPWRIGHT" — a turn-based platformer.

**Mechanics:**
- Grid-based levels (tiles). Player moves 1 tile per action: left, right, jump (up), or wait. Gravity applies automatically as part of the turn resolution? Hmm. Turn-based platformers need clear rules. Let me think.

Option A: Pure grid turn-based — like a puzzle: choose an action (←/→/↑jump/wait). Jump = move up 1 tile if there's floor below destination… actually let's do this: jump moves you up 2 tiles? That gets complicated.

Option B: "Action points": each turn, player can perform exactly one action: step left, step right, jump (up to height 2), drop down through a gap, or wait. After the player acts, all enemies take their turn and move toward the player (simple AI with pathing). Falling is automatic: if there's no floor below after moving, you fall until you land (this can be part of that same action — falling is free, but enemies still act after). Gravity resolution: after an action, apply gravity until grounded. This preserves the platformer feel while being turn-based.

Hazards: spikes, crumbling platforms (crumble 1 turn after player stands on them), moving enemies, patrolling sentries, "clockwork" gears that toggle bridges, exit door. Enemies:
- **Crawler**: moves horizontally toward player every enemy turn; dies if falls off? Or bounces back at walls.
- **Hopper**: hops in an arc (up 1, forward 1… grid hop).
- **Sentinel/Turret**: shoots a bolt along its row every N turns? Bullets move 1 tile per enemy turn — that's cool! A "ticking" bullet advances 1 tile per turn.

Turn counter: each level has a budget of steps? Or time pressure from an approaching "turn wave"? Simpler: no hard timer, but fewer moves = higher score. Score based on moves used and coins collected.

**Turn system details:**

Let me define tile behaviors precisely:

Jump rule: from grounded position (px,py), jump: if both tiles above (py-1 and py-2) are open (not solid/crumble/spike/rispike-extended… note: one-way passes through) → rise 2 with arc anim; else if py-1 open → rise 1; else blocked (no turn consumed, bump feedback). Alternative model that preserves physics feel: **actions are moves; gravity always applies after each action.** So "jump" = impulse upward — but with discrete turns, an impulse resolves instantly: you rise until… nothing stops it. Unless jump is: move up 1 tile, then gravity pulls you back down immediately (net zero) — pointless.

So the coherent model: **you're always standing on something; "jump" only makes sense as a leap onto higher ground.** Options:
(a) Jump = climb: if there's a ledge/step up to ≤2 tiles above and reachable (tile py-1 or py-2 is open and you land on top of solid at py-2/py-3… i.e., landing tile must be open with floor below), you can "vault" up onto it. So jump moves you up 1–2 tiles, but requires a floor under the destination (or destination itself being solid? no — destination must be open + floor below). If not, jump fails (no turn consumed) or… then to cross flat ground you just step. To climb a 2-tile wall: press jump → rise up 2 onto top of wall (landing tile is above the wall's second row; floor = wall top). Feels like a platformer "auto-precision vault".
(b) Jump with horizontal air control: consume a turn for "jump" that puts you in airborne state; then next 1–2 actions can be ←/→ while airborne (arc), gravity applies each action. This is more platformer-y but multi-action = confusing turns? Actually could be fun: jump gives you 1 free horizontal step ("air move") — i.e., pressing ↑ while grounded does up 1; then if you press ←/→ while airborne, you move forward and drop… getting complex.

I think (a) is cleaner and still reads as a platformer when animated with an arc + squash: the character leaps up onto ledges. Pits crossed via stepping stones / one-way platforms / crumble bridges. Verticality = vaulting onto higher ledges; can also "drop" from ledge to lower level with ↓ (free? no — dropping is an action that consumes a turn and resolves the fall). Actually, let me make drop-through and ledge-drop be actions (consuming a turn) — consistent: every deliberate action costs 1 turn; gravity after action is free.

So final ruleset (crisp, teachable):
- **Step (←/→):** move 1 tile sideways if open. If no floor below destination → you walk off and fall to next floor (fall is free). If target has enemy → death. If wall → bump, no turn consumed.
- **Vault (↑/Space/W):** leap up onto higher ground: check up 2 then up 1? Let's do this: destination = the highest reachable tile d in {py-2, py-1} such that tiles between are open AND floor exists below destination (solid/crumble/oneway/rising-spike-retracted? floor under dest must be a standable solid-ish; oneway counts as floor). Choose up 2 if valid, else up 1. If neither valid → bump (no turn consumed). Vault animates with a nice arc + stretch.
- **Drop/Wait (↓/S):** if standing on ONEMAY → drop through it (fall resolves). Else wait (turn passes; useful for rising-spike timing and turret cycles… enemies act after your wait so timing matters — e.g., stand where a bolt is coming; can you dodge? Bolt moves 1 per enemy turn, you move 1 per player turn — you can't outrun along the same row; but you can vault up or drop down to leave the row. Waiting lets you sync with rising spikes: if a spike is retracted and will extend next enemy turn, you must not be on it then — wait does nothing to save you… hmm, waiting's use: hopper pattern (hopper telegraphs crouch; if it can't reach you it stays), turret firing cycle (bolts spawn every 3rd turn; you might time your crossing so a bolt lands behind you? Bolts advance per enemy turn — after you step in, the bolt advances… dodging = being on a different tile when bolt arrives. Since both move 1/turn, relative velocity is 0 along row → can't dodge by running; must leave the row (vault/drop). Waiting's real use: let rising spikes pop while you're adjacent then cross? If spike extends during enemy phase and you're not on it, safe; retracts next player turn… no wait — parity: extended on odd global turns. Global turn increments each full round (player+enemy = 1 "turn"? or each side acts?). Let me define a "turn" as one full cycle: your action + foes' actions. RISPIKE extended when turn %2==0? Telegraphing makes it readable: during YOUR phase, spikes that will pop show a red pulse; you plan to not be there (or move). If you're on it during your phase and it pops during enemy phase → death. So waiting next to one: it pops (you safe), then next round it's retracted? No — extended on odd turns, retracts at… when does it retract? At start of that turn's player phase? Let me do this: state determined by parity; transitions happen at the end of enemy phase (pop) and at the start of player phase (retract)? That way it's only dangerous during enemy phase if you're on it → telegraphed. Simpler rule for player: "~ tiles are safe to stand on during your turn, but they pop while foes act — don't linger." Clean! So retracted at start of your phase, extended during enemy phase (every round!). Wait then it's just "you can never be on ~ when the enemy phase begins" → acts as a moving floor that's only usable transiently?? That makes ~ tiles nearly unusable except to cross in one action: step onto ~ during player phase (safe), enemy phase: it extends → you die. So ~ = effectively always a death trap. No good.

Better: true alternation: extended on even rounds, retracted on odd rounds. Transition at end of enemy phase (parity flips). During your phase on round N, state = parity(N). Alternative model that preserves physics feel: **actions are moves; gravity always applies after each action.** So "jump" = impulse upward — but with discrete turns, an impulse resolves instantly: you rise until… nothing stops it. Unless jump is: move up 1 tile, then gravity pulls you back down immediately (net zero) — pointless.

So the coherent model: **you're always standing on something; "jump" only makes sense as a leap onto higher ground.** Options:
(a) Jump = climb: if there's a ledge/step up to ≤2 tiles above and reachable (tile py-1 or py-2 is open and you land on top of solid at py-2/py-3… i.e., landing tile must be open with floor below), you can "vault" up onto it. So jump moves you up 1–2 tiles, but requires a floor under the destination (or destination itself being solid? no — destination must be open + floor below). If not, jump fails (no turn consumed) or… then to cross flat ground you just step. To climb a 2-tile wall: press jump → rise up 2 onto top of wall (landing tile is above the wall's second row; floor = wall top). Feels like a platformer "auto-precision vault".
(b) Jump with horizontal air control: consume a turn for "jump" that puts you in airborne state; then next 1–2 actions can be ←/→ while airborne (arc), gravity applies each action. This is more platformer-y but multi-action = confusing turns? Actually could be fun: jump gives you 1 free horizontal step ("air move") — i.e., pressing ↑ while grounded does up 1; then if you press ←/→ while airborne, you move forward and drop… getting complex.

I think (a) is cleaner and still reads as a platformer when animated with an arc + squash: the character leaps up onto ledges. Pits crossed via stepping stones / one-way platforms / crumble bridges. Verticality = vaulting onto higher ledges; can also "drop" from ledge to lower level with ↓ (free? no — dropping is an action that consumes a turn and resolves the fall). So final ruleset (crisp, teachable):
- **Step (←/→):** move 1 tile sideways if open. If no floor below destination → you walk off and fall to next floor (fall is free). If target has enemy → death. If wall → bump, no turn consumed.
- **Vault (↑/Space/W):** leap up onto higher ground: check up 2 then up 1? **Level names:** L1 "The Brass Steps", L2 "Ticking Hall", L3 "The Gearworks", L4 "Winder's Spire".

**Now code architecture (final):**

```js
const LEVELS = [ {name, par, map:[strings]} ×4 ];
Game state: levelIdx, grid (2D of tile codes), crumbleMarks Set("x,y"), entities: enemies[], bolts[], cogs[], particles[], floaters[], player{x,y,px,py(render floats),facing,squash,...}, turnCount, moves, phase, seq timeline, shake, cam.
```

Tile codes: 0 empty, 1 solid, 2 plate, 3 crumble, 4 spike, 5 door. (Parsers place entities separately.)

Helpers: inB(x,y), tileAt(x,y) (out of bounds → treat as solid for sides/bottom? Bottom = empty (fall death); sides/top = solid walls so you can't leave level horizontally — my levels have wall columns anyway; out-of-bounds: left/right/top → solid, bottom → empty).
floorUnder(x,y): t=tileAt(x,y+1): t===1||t===3||(t===2)→true; also… plate yes. Spike? No. Door? No (door cell has floor under it from level design ✓).
cellOpen(x,y): tile is 0 or 5(door) — plates are not open (can't occupy), crumble IS open/walkable (it's a floor surface: you stand ON it → occupy the cell above; can you occupy a crumble cell itself? Crumble occupies the floor row like solid: walking on bridge = occupying row above ✓ so crumble cell is NOT open (like solid). Plate cell not open. So open = 0 or 5.)
Enemy occupancy map for movement checks.

Player action handler (phase==='player'):
- dir left/right: facing=dir; tx=x+dir; if !cellOpen(tx,y) → bump (if enemy at → still die? Walking into enemy: allow → death. So: if enemyAt(tx,ty) → move there & die (dramatic). Else if !open → bump, return (no turn).)
  Else doAction(move to tx, same y; then gravity resolve).
- up/space: facing as is; try vault h2 then h1 in facing dir: for h in [2,1]: lx=x+facing, ly=y-h; valid = cellOpen(lx,ly) && floorUnder(lx,ly) && (h===2 ? cellOpen(lx,y-1) : true); also ensure path cells between are open: for k=1..h: cellOpen(x+facing, y-k)… that's the same as lx column ✓. If valid → doAction(vault anim to (lx,ly)). Also require no enemy at landing (else die — allow? Vaulting into enemy = death, allow for consistency).
  If neither valid → bump, no turn.
- down: if floorUnder(x,y) is a PLATE (tileAt(x,y+1)===2) → doAction(drop: pass through: set y+=1 then gravity). Else wait (doAction wait: small idle anim, counts as move? Yes — waiting consumes the turn; moves++ ✓ show "you wait" in ticker occasionally).

doAction(action): moves++; phase='pAnim'; play tick; animate player render position to target over duration (tween list); onComplete → resolveGravity() → after fall complete → postResolve(): check door (clear), spikes (die), enemy overlap (die — e.g., hopped onto you? that's enemy phase; here: vaulted into enemy), mark crumble under feet, then startEnemyPhase().

resolveGravity(): if floorUnder(x,y) → done. Else fall: compute targetY: let ty=y; while(ty+1<H && !floorUnder(x,ty)) ty++; (H = grid height; if ty reaches H-1 and still no floor → fell out: death at y=H+1). Animate from current renderY to ty with easeInQuad, duration 90*dist + 80. During fall, check stomp: for each enemy e where e.x===x && e.y is in (y..ty] and player's renderY crosses e.y-0.2 while descending → stomp(e). After landing at ty: if tileAt(x,ty)===4 spike → die; if enemy at (x,ty) → die (or stomp? If enemy is exactly where you land = side collision → die… actually if an enemy occupies your landing cell and you fell onto it from above → that's a stomp! Distinguish: if fall distance ≥1 and enemy at landing cell → stomp ✓; else die.) Then continue.

startEnemyPhase(): phase='eAnim'; HUD flip (FOES); seq timeline:
  let t=0; for each enemy i: t+=95; seq.push([t, ()=>enemyAct(e)]);
  after enemies: turretFire(): for turrets where cycle matches → spawn bolt + flash (at time t+60).
  bolts: t+=120; seq.push([t, stepBolts]).
  crumble break: t+=80; seq.push([t, breakCrumble]) — if any tile under player broke → player falls: resolveGravity again (chain) then hazards.
  final: t+=90; seq.push([t, endEnemyPhase]): check deaths (bolt overlap, crawler-on-player handled in enemyAct via landing on player cell → set dead flag), else turnCount++, phase='player', HUD flip, tick sound, update ticker.

enemyAct(e):
- Crawler: dir=sign(player.x-e.x)||e.face; tx=e.x+dir; if (tx===player.x&&e.y===player.y) → move onto player → death("The Mite caught you"). Else valid = cellOpen(tx,e.y)&&floorUnder(tx,e.y)&&tileAt(tx,e.y)!==3&&!enemyAt(tx,e.y); if valid → tween e render pos 120ms, set e.x=tx immediately (logic first), face=dir.
- Hopper: dir toward player; for s in [2,1]: tx=e.x+dir*s; if tx===player.x&&e.y===player.y → jump there (death "The Flicker landed on you") break. valid = cellOpen&&!crumble&&!enemyAt&&floorUnder… plates block enemies: tileAt(tx,e.y) must be 0 or 5. If found → crouch anim then arc tween to (tx, e.y), set e.x=tx.
- Turret: nothing per turn (fires on cycle).

stepBolts(): for each bolt: nx=b.x+b.dir; if !cellOpen(nx,b.y)?? Bolts blocked by solid/crumble/plate?? Plates: pass through ✓ so block = tile 1 or 3 only (and walls); spikes: bolt hits spike → both die? Let bolt vanish on spike too. If blocked → remove + sparks. Else b.x=nx; tween; if b.x===player.x&&b.y===player.y → death("Struck by a cog-bolt"); ttl--; if ttl<=0 remove.

breakCrumble(): for each mark: grid=0, particles, sound, ticker "The bridge gives way"; clear marks; if player's floorUnder was one of them (i.e., !floorUnder(player)) → resolveGravity chain (careful with sequencing — run after break, before endEnemyPhase).

checkPlayerHazards() called at key points.

Death: phase='dead'; explosion particles at player pos; shake 14; sound; after 650ms show overlay (DOM) with reason + moves; R or button → loadLevel(levelIdx) (reset state, keep best).

Clear: phase='clear'; door burst; iris animation (canvas draws expanding circle); sound arpeggio; after ~900ms show DOM panel: stats (moves, par, delta, cogs x/y, best moves for level, score contribution); Enter/button → next level or win screen. Score: cogs×100 + max(0,(par-moves))*15 + 250.

Win: total score, sum of moves, total cogs, rating text by score thresholds; Play Again → loadLevel(0), reset totals… keep per-run totals in memory; best per level in localStorage.

**HUD DOM:**
Top-left: `LV 1/4 · The Brass Steps` (name in display font small).
Top-center: turn chip: [YOU ▮▮▮ | FOES] — implement as two segments with active glow + label text swap "YOUR MOVE"/"FOES ACT".
Top-right: `MOVES 07` and cogs `⚙ 1/3`.
Bottom-left ticker. Bottom-right: hint keys mini (←→ move · ↑ leap · ↓ drop/wait · R restart · M sound).
All DOM, pointer-events none except buttons.

**Pause:** P toggles overlay; also auto-pause on blur? Nice touch: window blur → if playing, pause. Keep simple: manual + Enter to resume.

**Start screen content:** Title "STEPWRIGHT", tagline "a turn-by-turn platformer — every step costs a tick of the clock". Controls list with kbd caps. Button "BEGIN THE CLIMB [ENTER]". Small note: "M sound · R restart level · P pause". Behind: live render of L1 with slow camera drift (phase 'menu': run ambient loop, no enemies acting? Just idle embers + player blinking in place — cute: player does an idle bob).

**Canvas sizing:** canvas fills window; T (tile px) = clamp(min(W/22, H/15.5), 20, 64)? Ensure level fits: view shows ~W/T × H/T tiles. Camera clamps; if level smaller than view → center offset. DPR scaling via ctx.setTransform(dpr*...).

**Seeded random for skyline:** mulberry32 per level idx.

**Performance:** visible tile culling, particle cap 400, shadowBlur used sparingly (only small items). Should be fine.

**Fonts CSS:**

**Audio implementation:**
```js
let AC=null, master, muted=false(from storage);
function audio(){ if(!AC){AC=new (window.AudioContext||webkitAudioContext)(); master=AC.createGain(); master.gain.value=0.5; master.connect(AC.destination);} if(AC.state==='suspended')AC.resume(); }
function tone(f0,f1,dur,type,vol,delay=0){...osc+gain envelope...}
function noise(dur,vol,filterFreq){...}
SFX = { tick(){tone(340,300,.05,'square',.25)}, tock(){tone(210,180,.06,'square',.22)}, jump(){tone(300,560,.12,'sine',.3)}, land(){noise(.07,.2,900); tone(140,90,.08,'triangle',.25)}, coin(){tone(880,880,.06,'sine',.3); tone(1318,1318,.09,'sine',.28,.06)}, crumble(){noise(.25,.35,400)}, death(){tone(220,55,.45,'sawtooth',.35); noise(.3,.3,600)}, stomp(){tone(300,90,.12,'triangle',.35)}, door(){[523,659,784,1046].forEach((f,i)=>tone(f,f,.12,'sine',.28,i*.07))}, bump(){tone(120,90,.06,'square',.2)}, bolt(){tone(700,300,.08,'sawtooth',.15)}, ui(){tone(660,660,.04,'square',.2)} };
```
First input (keydown/click) → audio() init. M toggles master gain 0/0.5 + persist.

**Sequencer:**
```js
let seq=[], seqT0=0;
function schedule(list){ seq=list; seqT0=performance.now(); }
in update(dt): const t=now-seqT0; while(seq.length&&seq[0][0]<=t){seq.shift()[1]();}
```
Careful: fns may push new items → keep seq as array sorted by time, simpler: maintain array, each frame run all with time<=t (mark done). Implement: seq = [{at,fn,done}].

**Player render:** px,py floats in tile units; tween via a small animator:
```js
function tween(obj, from, to, dur, ease, onDone) — manage in an active list processed each frame.
```
Simpler: player has rx,ry (render), and I set p.anim = {t0,dur,fx,fy,tx,ty,type} per action; update computes eased pos + squash params by type ('step','vault','fall','drop','wait'). Squash: vault → stretch (sy 1.25,sx .8) at start, land → squash (sy .7). Use a spring-ish decay after landing: p.squash decays to 0.

Enemies similar minimal anim (rx lerp to x with per-type easing; hopper uses arc param during its jump anim: store e.anim {t0,dur,fx,fy,tx}).

**Camera:** cam.x += (targetX-cam.x)*min(1,dt*8); clamp.

**Draw order:** sky gradient → gear-moon → skyline → mist band → (world translate -cam*T + shake) tiles → cogs → door glow → enemies → bolts → player → particles → floaters → turn tint overlay → vignette. Iris wipe during clear drawn last in screen space.

**Turn tint:** if phase eAnim: overlay fillRect rgba(255,80,60, .05 + pulse*.03).

**Vignette:** radial gradient each frame (cache to offscreen on resize for perf — or just draw; fine).

**Floating texts:** {x,y,txt,t0} rise & fade 900ms, mono bold, brass color.

**Best storage:**
```js
function loadSave(){ try{ return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('stepwright1'))||{} }catch(e){return {}} }
function saveBest(i,m){ try{ const s=loadSave(); if(!s.best[i]||m<s.best[i]) s.best[i]=m; localStorage.setItem('stepwright1',JSON.stringify(s)); }catch(e){} }
```
Mute also persisted in same object.

**Restart (R):** works during play & death & clear? R = restart current level anytime (except menu/win). Implement loadLevel(idx) fully re-init.

**Pause:** overlay with "PAUSED — P to resume, R restart".

Edge cases:
- Input during pAnim/eAnim → ignore (maybe queue 1 buffered input for snappiness? Add small input buffer: if pressed during last 150ms of anim… skip, keep strict; turn-based = deliberate).
- Multiple keys same frame: process first only.
- Vault when facing into a wall but space up open? Facing-based → bump. Fine.
- Player at top row? Levels have ceiling row 0 solid ✓.
- Hopper dir when player is directly above/below (same x): sign=0 → keep last face; if same x and different y: hopper can't vertical… just stays/crouch. Crawler same x: stay. OK.
- Bolt vs plate pass-through ✓; bolt vs door cell: passes? Door cell open → passes, fine.
- Stomp chain: stomping sets enemy dead (remove), particle burst, +150 floater, sound.
- Two enemies same cell prevented by enemyAt checks.
- Crawler on raised end (L1): floorUnder uses tile ✓; crawler refuses crumble/plate cells ✓ stays in zone.
- Hopper landing onto player → death flag: implement as e.x=player.x then checkPlayerHazards after each enemyAct? Run hazard check after all enemies + bolts (single point) — but anim stagger means visual lag; acceptable: mark dead immediately, show overlay after phase ends (or immediately with 500ms delay). Better: if death triggered mid-enemy-phase, cancel remaining seq and go to death sequence right away. Implement `die(reason)` that sets phase='dead', clears seq.

- Door trigger only during postResolve (player's own action) — can't die/door during enemy phase ✓.

Rating by total score: thresholds → "Apprentice Winder" / "Journeyman of Steps" / "Master of the Clockwork".

function tileAt(x,y){ if(y>=G.H) return 0; if(x<0||x>=G.W||y<0) return 1; return G.grid[y][x]; }

function isFloor(t){return t===T_SOLID||t===T_CRUMB||t===T_PLATE;}
function floorUnder(x,y){ return isFloor(tileAt(x,y+1)) || enemyAt(x,y+1)===G.turretList?? }

Player move validation:
```js
function stepMove(dir){ const p=G.player; p.face=dir; const tx=p.x+dir, ty=p.y;
  if(tx<0||tx>=G.W) return bump();
  const e=enemyAt(tx,ty);
  if(!cellOpen(tx,ty)){ if(e){ moveToPlayerDeath? } else return bump(); }
  // allow moving into enemy (death) or open
  doAction({type:'step', tx, ty});
}
```
If target has enemy but cell is open → move → death check in postResolve ("You walked into a Mite"). If target not open AND enemy there?? Enemy on non-open cell impossible. So: if !cellOpen → bump (no turn). Else doAction; postResolve checks enemyAt(newpos) → die.

vault():
```js
function vault(){ const p=G.player; const d=p.face; let ok=false;
  for(const h of [2,1]){ const lx=p.x+d, ly=p.y-h;
    if(lx<0||lx>=G.W||ly<0) continue;
    if(!cellOpen(lx,ly)) continue;
    if(h===2 && !cellOpen(p.x+d,p.y-1)) continue;
    if(floorUnder(lx,ly)){ ok=true; p.vaultH=h; doAction({type:'vault',tx:lx,ty:ly}); return; }
  }
  bump();
}
```
Wait h2 path check: cells (x+d, y-1) and (x+d, y-2=ly): the loop checks cellOpen(lx,ly) for ly=y-2 ✓ and intermediate (x+d,y-1) ✓.

startFall():
```js
const p=G.player; let ty=p.y, guard=0;
while(ty+1<G.H && !floorUnder(p.x,ty) && guard++<G.H){ ty++; }
if(!floorUnder(p.x,ty)){ // fell out bottom
  p.fallOut=true; animate render to H+2 then die('You fell into the dark'); return; }
const dist=ty-p.y; p.anim={t0:now(),dur:90*dist+120,type:'fall',fx:p.rx,fy:p.ry,fromY:p.y,toY:ty};
G.stompCheck={from:p.y,to:ty,x:p.x};
onDone → p.y=ty; resolve stomp (if any enemy was in column between); then landing checks.
```

startEnemyPhase():
```js
G.phase='eAnim'; hudSetFoe(); SFX.tock();
const t0=now(); let t=120; const items=[];
for(const e of G.enemies){ if(e.dead) continue; const et=t; items.push([et, ()=>enemyAct(e)]); t+= (e.type==='hop'? 260 : 150); }
items.push([t+40, fireTurrets]);
t+=130; items.push([t, stepBolts]);
t+=90; items.push([t, breakCrumble]);
t+=110; items.push([t, endEnemyPhase]);
G.seq=items.map(([at,fn])=>({at:t0+at,fn}));
```

enemyAct(e):
- if e.anim active skip (stagger ensures done).
- Crawler: if(Math.abs(p.y-e.y)>1) { e.asleep visual; return; } dir = p.x>e.x?1:p.x<e.x?-1:e.face; tx=e.x+dir;
  if(tx===p.x&&e.y===p.y){ e.x=tx; e.anim small lunge; die('The Mite reached you'); return; }
  if(cellOpen(tx,e.y)&&floorUnder(tx,e.y)&&!enemyAt(tx,e.y)){ e.face=dir; setPos anim 140ms; e.x=tx; } else e.face=dir;
- Hopper: dir toward player (if same x → e.face); for s of [2,1]: tx=e.x+dir*s; if(tx===p.x&&e.y===p.y){ arc anim to there; die('The Flicker landed on you'); return;} if(inB(tx)&&cellOpen(tx,e.y)&&floorUnder(tx,e.y)&&!enemyAt(tx,e.y)){ e.x=tx; hopAnim; ticker occasionally; break; }
  else crouch anim.
- Turret: nothing (fires handled globally).

die(reason): if(G.phase==='dead')return; G.phase='dead'; G.reason=reason; SFX.death(); shake=16; burst particles at player; p.dead=true (hide player, show shards); after 700ms (setTimeout OK? use G.seq or time check in update — I'll use a timestamp and check in loop) → show deathOv with text.

Input:
```js
window.addEventListener('keydown', e=>{
  const k=e.key;
  audioInit(); // first input
  const gameKeys=['ArrowLeft','ArrowRight','ArrowUp','ArrowDown',' ','Spacebar'];
  if(gameKeys.includes(k)) e.preventDefault();
  if(e.repeat) return;
  if(k==='m'||k==='M'){ toggleMute(); return; }
  if(G.phase==='menu'){ if(k==='Enter') startRun(); return; }
  if(G.phase==='win'){ if(k==='Enter') restartAll(); return; }
  if(G.phase==='dead'){ if(k==='r'||k==='R'||k==='Enter') retryLevel(); return; }
  if(G.phase==='clearPending'){ if(k==='Enter') nextLevel(); return; } // clearOv open
  if(k==='p'||k==='P'||k==='Escape'){ togglePause(); return; }
  if(G.paused){ if(k==='p'...) handled above; return; }
  if(G.phase==='player'){
    if(k==='r'||k==='R'){ retryLevel(); return; }
    switch(k){ case 'ArrowLeft': case 'a': case 'A': stepMove(-1); break; ... case 'ArrowUp': case 'w': case 'W': case ' ': vault(); break; case 'ArrowDown': case 's': case 'S': dropOrWait(); break; }
  }
});
```

```

    <div class="footNote">sound starts after your first key · best steps are remembered on this device</div>
  </div>
</div>

<script>
"use strict";
/* ================= helpers ================= */
const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
const clamp=(v,a,b)=>v<a?a:v>b?b:v;
function mulberry(s){return function(){s|=0;s=s+0x6D2B79F5|0;let t=Math.imul(s^s>>>15,1|s);t=t+Math.imul(t^t>>>7,61|t)^t;return((t^t>>>14)>>>0)/4294967296}}
function hash2(x,y){let h=(x*374761393+y*668265263)|0;h=(h^(h>>13))|0;h=Math.imul(h,1274126177);return (h^(h>>16))>>>0}

/* ================= audio ================= */
let AC=null,master=null;
function ensureAudio(){try{
  if(!AC){const C=window.AudioContext||window.webkitAudioContext;if(!C)return;AC=new C();master=AC.createGain();master.gain.value=G.muted?0:0.5;master.connect(AC.destination);}
  if(AC.state==="suspended")AC.resume();
}catch(e){}}
function tone(f0,f1,dur,type,vol,delay){if(!AC||G.muted)return;try{
  const t0=AC.currentTime+(delay||0),o=AC.createOscillator(),g=AC.createGain();
  o.type=type;o.frequency.setValueAtTime(Math.max(20,f0),t0);o.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(Math.max(20,f1),t0+dur);
  g.gain.setValueAtTime(vol,t0);g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001,t0+dur);
  o.connect(g);g.connect(master);o.start(t0);o.stop(t0+dur+.02);}catch(e){}}
function noiseBurst(dur,vol,freq,delay){if(!AC||G.muted)return;try{
  const t0=AC.currentTime+(delay||0),n=Math.floor(AC.sampleRate*dur),buf=AC.createBuffer(1,n,AC.sampleRate),d=buf.getChannelData(0);
  for(let i=0;i<n;i++)d[i]=(Math.random()*2-1)*(1-i/n);
  const s=AC.createBufferSource(),f=AC.createBiquadFilter(),g=AC.createGain();
  s.buffer=buf;f.type="lowpass";f.frequency.value=freq;g.gain.value=vol;
  s.connect(f);f.connect(g);g.connect(master);s.start(t0);}catch(e){}}
const SFX={
  tick:()=>tone(340,290,.05,"square",.18), tock:()=>tone(215,175,.06,"square",.16),
  jump:()=>tone(300,560,.12,"sine",.26), land:()=>{noiseBurst(.06,.14,900);tone(150,90,.07,"triangle",.2)},
  coin:()=>{tone(880,880,.06,"sine",.26);tone(1318,1318,.1,"sine",.22,.06)},
  crumble:()=>noiseBurst(.28,.3,420), death:()=>{tone(220,52,.5,"sawtooth",.3);noiseBurst(.32,.28,600)},
  stomp:()=>tone(300,90,.13,"triangle",.32), bump:()=>tone(120,85,.06,"square",.16),
  door:()=>[523,659,784,1046].forEach((f,i)=>tone(f,f,.14,"sine",.24,i*.08)),
  bolt:()=>tone(720,280,.09,"sawtooth",.12), ui:()=>tone(660,660,.045,"square",.16)
};

/* ================= level data ================= */
const LEVELS=[
{name:"The Brass Steps",par:34,start:[2,10],sky:["#20284c","#4a3d6e","#b25e47"],map:[
"########################################",

{name:"Ticking Hall",par:46,start:[2,10],sky:["#1c2344","#3d3466","#8f4f63"],map:[

#"#..............c...........................#",

{name:"The Gearworks",par:44,start:[2,13],sky:["#141d3d","#2f3260","#5f4a7e"],map:[
"############################################",
"#..........................................#",
"#...................................c......#",

{name:"Winder's Spire",par:52,start:[2,13],sky:["#0f1733","#273059","#b0653f"],map:[
"###############################################",
"#...............................................#",

"#.....c...........................................",
"#...............................................#",
"#.....t..#.....................................#",
"######################^^^^^^^^#########################"]],
};
/* NOTE: rows above were authored by hand; a normalizer guarantees uniform width & height. */

const T_EMPTY=0,T_SOLID=1,T_PLATE=2,T_CRUMB=3,T_SPIKE=4,T_DOOR=5;

/* ================= state ================= */
const G={phase:"menu",paused:false,t:0,levelIdx:0,grid:[],W:0,H:0,enemies:[],bolts:[],cogs:[],particles:[],floaters:[],
  marks:new Set(),extra:new Set(),player:null,cam:{x:0,y:0},shake:0,moves:0,turns:1,foeCount:0,cogsGot:0,
  seq:[],act:null,pending:null,reason:"",deadAt:0,clearAt:0,ov:null,score:0,run:null,skyline:[],seed:7,
  muted:false,demo:true,T:44,vw:0,vh:0};

function loadSave(){try{return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("stepwright1"))||{}}catch(e){return {}}}
function persist(patch){try{const s=loadSave();Object.assign(s,patch);localStorage.setItem("stepwright1",JSON.stringify(s));}catch(e){}}
(function initMute(){const s=loadSave();if(s.muted)G.muted=true;})();

/* ================= geometry rules ================= */
function tileAt(x,y){if(y>=G.H)return 0;if(x<0||x>=G.W||y<0)return T_SOLID;return G.grid[y][x];}
const isFloor=t=>t===T_SOLID||t===T_CRUMB||t===T_PLATE;
function floorUnder(x,y){if(isFloor(tileAt(x,y+1)))return true;return G.extra.has(x+","+y+1);}
function cellOpen(x,y){const t=tileAt(x,y);return t===T_EMPTY||t===T_DOOR;}
function enemyAt(x,y){for(const e of G.enemies)if(!e.dead&&e.x===x&&e.y===y)return e;return null;}

/* ================= level build ================= */
function normalizeMap(m){const w=Math.max(...m.map(r=>r.length)),h=m.length;return m.map(r=>r.padEnd(w," ").slice(0,w));}
function buildLevel(i,demo){
  const L=LEVELS[i];G.levelIdx=i;G.demo=demo;
  const rows=normalizeMap(L.map);G.W=rows[0].length;G.H=rows.length;
  G.grid=[];G.enemies=[];G.bolts=[];G.cogs=[];G.particles=[];G.floaters=[];G.marks.clear();G.extra.clear();
  for(let y=0;y<G.H;y++){const row=[];for(let x=0;x<G.W;x++){const ch=rows[y][x];let t=T_EMPTY;
    switch(ch){case "#":t=T_SOLID;break;case "=":t=T_PLATE;break;case "*":t=T_CRUMB;break;case "^":t=T_SPIKE;break;case "D":t=T_DOOR;break;}
    if(ch==="c")G.cogs.push({x,y,taken:false});
    if(ch==="m")G.enemies.push(mkEnemy("crawl",x,y));
    if(ch==="f")G.enemies.push(mkEnemy("hop",x,y));
    if(ch==="t"){G.enemies.push(mkEnemy("turret",x,y,1));}
    if(ch==="T"){G.enemies.push(mkEnemy("turret",x,y,-1));}
    row.push(t);}G.grid.push(row);}
  for(const e of G.enemies)if(e.type==="turret")G.extra.add(e.x+","+e.y);
  const p=L.start;G.player={x:p[0],y:p[1],rx:p[0],ry:p[1],face:1,anim:null,sq:0,dead:false};
  G.moves=0;G.turns=1;G.foeCount=0;G.cogsGot=0;G.seq=[];G.act=null;G.pending=null;G.shake=0;
  G.cam.x=p[0];G.cam.y=p[1]-4;
  // skyline (deterministic per level)
  const rnd=mulberry(1234+i*77);G.skyline=[];let sx=-2;
  while(sx<G.W+8){const w=2+rnd()*5|0,hh=2+rnd()*6|0;G.skyline.push({x:sx,w,h:hh,cog:rnd()>.45});sx+=w+rnd()*2;}
  G.phase="player";G.ov=demo?"menu":null;syncOverlays();updateHUD();hudPhase("you",900);
  if(!demo)ticker("LV "+(i+1)+" — "+L.name+" · reach the glowing door");
}
function mkEnemy(type,x,y,dir){return {type,x,y,rx:x,ry:y,face:dir||-1,anim:null,crouch:0,dead:false};}

/* ================= HUD / ticker / overlays ================= */
function updateHUD(){const L=LEVELS[G.levelIdx];
  $("lvlTag").textContent="LV "+(G.levelIdx+1)+"/"+LEVELS.length;
  $("lvlName").textContent=L.name;
  $("movesVal").textContent=String(G.moves).padStart(2,"0");
  $("cogVal").textContent=G.cogsGot+"/"+G.cogs.length;
  $("turnNo").textContent="TURN "+String(G.turns).padStart(2,"0");}
function hudPhase(who,dur){const y=$("youSeg"),f=$("foeSeg"),fill=$("phaseFill");
  y.classList.toggle("on",who==="you");f.classList.toggle("on",who==="foe");
  fill.style.transition="none";fill.style.width="0";void fill.offsetWidth;
  fill.style.background=who==="you"?"var(--amber)":"var(--red)";
  fill.style.transition="width "+dur+"ms linear";fill.style.width="100%";}
let tickTimer=null;
function ticker(msg){const el=$("ticker"),tx=$("tickText");tx.textContent=msg;el.classList.remove("show");void el.offsetWidth;el.classList.add("show");}
function syncOverlays(){const map={menu:"menuOv",pause:"pauseOv",death:"deathOv",clear:"clearOv",win:"winOv"};
  for(const k in map)$(map[k]).classList.toggle("hidden",G.ov!==k);
  document.body.classList.toggle("inmenu",G.ov==="menu");}

/* ================= actions ================= */
function bump(){SFX.bump();const p=G.player;p.anim={t0:G.t,dur:140,type:"bump",fx:p.rx,fy:p.ry,dir:p.face};}
function doAction(a){if(G.phase!=="player")return;G.moves++;G.phase="pAnim";SFX.tick();updateHUD();hudPhase("you",700);
  const p=G.player;p.anim={t0:G.t,dur:a.type==="step"?150:a.type==="vault"?300:a.type==="drop"?90:340,type:a.type,fx:p.rx,fy:p.ry,h:a.h||0};
  if(a.type==="vault")SFX.jump();
  G.act=a;}
function stepMove(dir){const p=G.player;p.face=dir;const tx=p.x+dir,ty=p.y;
  if(tx<0||tx>=G.W)return bump();
  const t=tileAt(tx,ty);
  if(t===T_SPIKE){doAction({type:"step",tx,ty});return;}         // walk into spikes: dramatic
  if(!cellOpen(tx,ty))return bump();                              // wall / plate / crumble face
  doAction({type:"step",tx,ty});}
function vault(){const p=G.player,d=p.face;
  for(const h of [2,1]){const lx=p.x+d,ly=p.y-h;
    if(lx<0||lx>=G.W||ly<0)continue;
    if(!cellOpen(lx,ly))continue;
    if(h===2&&!cellOpen(p.x+d,p.y-1))continue;
    if(floorUnder(lx,ly)){doAction({type:"vault",tx:lx,ty:ly,h});return;}}
  bump();}
function dropOrWait(){const p=G.player;
  if(tileAt(p.x,p.y+1)===T_PLATE){doAction({type:"drop",tx:p.x,ty:p.y+1});}
  else{doAction({type:"wait"});if(Math.random()<.5)ticker("You hold your breath.");}}

function finalizeAction(){const a=G.act;G.act=null;const p=G.player;if(G.phase!=="pAnim")return;
  if(a.type==="wait"){afterPlayer();return;}
  p.x=a.tx;p.y=a.ty;
  collectCog();
  if(tileAt(p.x,p.y)===T_DOOR){levelClear();return;}
  const e=enemyAt(p.x,p.y);if(e){die(nameOf(e.type)+" got you");return;}
  if(!floorUnder(p.x,p.y)){startFall(true);}else{landFX(a.type==="vault");markCrumble();afterPlayer();}}

function collectCog(){const p=G.player;
  for(const c of G.cogs){if(c.taken)continue;if(c.x===p.x&&(c.y===p.y||c.y===p.y-1)){
    c.taken=true;G.cogsGot++;SFX.coin();updateHUD();
    addFloater(p.x,p.y-.6,"+100","#ffd27a");
    burst(p.x+.5,(p.y+.4),10,"#f0b45a",.9);}}}

function markCrumble(){const p=G.player;if(tileAt(p.x,p.y+1)===T_CRUMB)G.marks.add(p.x+","+ (p.y+1));}
function landFX(kind){const p=G.player;p.sq=1;SFX.land();
  burst(p.x+.5,p.y+1,kind==="vault"?7:4,"#9aa6d0",.5);}

/* ---- gravity / stomp ---- */
function startFall(fromPlayerAction){const p=G.player;let ty=p.y,g=0;
  while(g++<G.H&&ty+1<G.H&&!floorUnder(p.x,ty)&&tileAt(p.x,ty)!==T_SPIKE)ty++;
  const out=!floorUnder(p.x,ty)&&tileAt(p.x,ty)!==T_SPIKE&&ty===G.H-1;
  G.stomp={x:p.x,from:p.y,to:out?ty:ty};
  p.anim={t0:G.t,dur:out?420:90*(ty-p.y)+130,type:"fall",fx:p.rx,fy:p.ry,fromY:p.y,toY:out?G.H+1.6:ty,out};
  G.pending={fn:()=>{
    if(p.anim&&p.anim.out){die("You fell into the dark");return;}
    p.y=p.anim.toY;
    // stomp anything passed through from above
    for(const e of G.enemies){if(!e.dead&&e.type!=="turret"&&e.x===G.stomp.x&&e.y>p.anim.fromY-0.5&&e.y<=p.anim.toY+0.5){stomp(e);}}
    if(tileAt(p.x,p.y)===T_SPIKE){die("Impaled on spikes");return;}
    const e=enemyAt(p.x,p.y);if(e){die(nameOf(e.type)+" caught you mid-fall");return;}
    landFX("fall");markCrumble();
    if(fromPlayerAction)afterPlayer(); /* else: enemy-phase settle, round already ends via seq */
  }};
}
function stomp(e){e.dead=true;SFX.stomp();G.shake=Math.max(G.shake,5);
  addFloater(e.x,e.y-.4,"+150","#57d9a3");burst(e.x+.5,e.y+.5,16,colorOf(e.type),1.2);
  ticker("Stomped the "+nameOf(e.type)+"!");}

/* ---- enemy phase ---- */
function afterPlayer(){if(G.phase==="dead")return;G.phase="eAnim";SFX.tock();hudPhase("foe",G.enemies.filter(e=>!e.dead).length*170+620);
  let t=130;const items=[];
  for(const e of G.enemies){if(e.dead)continue;items.push([t,()=>enemyAct(e)]);t+=e.type==="hop"?290:165;}
  items.push([t+40,fireTurrets]);t+=140;items.push([t,stepBolts]);t+=95;items.push([t,breakCrumble]);t+=120;
  G.seq=items.map(([at,fn])=>({at:G.t+at,fn}));}
function nameOf(t){return t==="crawl"?"Mite":t==="hop"?"Flicker":"Cog-Turret";}
function colorOf(t){return t==="crawl"?"#ff6b6b":t==="hop"?"#57d9a3":"#8fa3c8";}

function enemyAct(e){if(G.phase!=="eAnim"||e.dead)return;const p=G.player;
  if(e.type==="crawl"){
    if(Math.abs(p.y-e.y)>1){e.asleep=true;return;} e.asleep=false;
    if(G.foeCount%2===1)return;                       // sluggish: every other tick
    const dir=p.x>e.x?1:p.x<e.x?-1:e.face;e.face=dir;const tx=e.x+dir;
    if(tx===p.x&&e.y===p.y){setE(e,tx,e.y,140,"lunge");die("The Mite reached you");return;}
    if(cellOpen(tx,e.y)&&floorUnder(tx,e.y)&&!enemyAt(tx,e.y))setE(e,tx,e.y,150,"walk");
  }else if(e.type==="hop"){
    const dir=p.x>e.x?1:p.x<e.x?-1:e.face;e.face=dir;let did=false;
    for(const s of [2,1]){const tx=e.x+dir*s;
      if(tx===p.x&&e.y===p.y){setE(e,tx,e.y,340,"hop");die("The Flicker landed on you");did=true;break;}
      if(tx<0||tx>=G.W)continue;
      if(cellOpen(tx,e.y)&&floorUnder(tx,e.y)&&!enemyAt(tx,e.y)){setE(e,tx,e.y,360,"hop");did=true;break;}}
    if(!did){e.crouch=1;}
  }}
function setE(e,tx,ty,dur,type){e.anim={t0:G.t,dur,fx:e.rx,fy:e.ry,type};e.x=tx;e.y=ty;}

function fireTurrets(){if(G.phase!=="eAnim")return;const will=(G.foeCount+1)%3===0;if(!will)return;
  for(const e of G.enemies){if(e.type!=="turret"||e.dead)continue;
    const nx=e.x+e.face;if(nx<0||nx>=G.W)continue;
    if(tileAt(nx,e.y)===T_EMPTY||tileAt(nx,e.y)===T_DOOR){
      G.bolts.push({x:nx,y:e.y,rx:nx,ry:e.y,dir:e.face,life:14,moved:false,anim:null});
      SFX.bolt();burst(nx+.5,e.y+.5,5,"#ffd27a",.6);ticker("A cog-turret fires!");}}}
function stepBolts(){if(G.phase!=="eAnim")return;const p=G.player;
  for(const b of G.bolts){if(b.dead)continue;
    const nx=b.x+b.dir,t=tileAt(nx,b.y);
    if(t===T_SOLID||t===T_CRUMB||t===T_SPIKE||G.extra.has(nx+","+b.y)||nx<0||nx>=G.W){b.dead=true;burst(nx+.5,b.y+.5,7,"#ffd27a",.7);continue;}
    b.x=nx;b.anim={t0:G.t,dur:120,fx:b.rx,fy:b.ry};b.moved=true;b.life--;
    if(b.life<=0){b.dead=true;burst(b.x+.5,b.y+.5,4,"#8b95bd",.5);continue;}
    if(!b.firstHit){b.firstHit=true;} else if(b.x===p.x&&b.y===p.y){die("Struck by a cog-bolt");return;}}}
function breakCrumble(){if(G.phase!=="eAnim")return;let any=false,underP=false;const p=G.player;
  for(const k of G.marks){const [x,y]=k.split(",").map(Number);G.grid[y][x]=T_EMPTY;any=true;
    burst(x+.5,y+.5,10,"#a4713f",.9);if(p.x===x&&p.y===y-1)underP=true;}
  if(!any)return;G.marks.clear();SFX.crumble();ticker("The cracked stone gives way!");
  if(underP&&!floorUnder(p.x,p.y))startFall(false);}

function endEnemyPhase(){if(G.phase!=="eAnim")return;G.foeCount++;G.turns++;G.phase="player";updateHUD();SFX.tick();hudPhase("you",900);}

/* ================= death / clear / win ================= */
function die(reason){if(G.phase==="dead"||G.phase==="clear")return;G.phase="dead";G.reason=reason;G.seq=[];G.pending=null;G.act=null;
  const p=G.player;p.dead=true;SFX.death();G.shake=16;
  burst(p.rx+.5,p.ry+.4,26,"#ffd27a",1.6);burst(p.rx+.5,p.ry+.4,14,"#ff8d7e",1.2);
  G.deadAt=G.t;}
function levelClear(){if(G.phase==="clear")return;G.phase="clear";G.seq=[];G.pending=null;SFX.door();
  const p=G.player;burst(p.x+.5,p.y+.3,30,"#ffd27a",1.5);G.shake=6;G.clearAt=G.t;G.iris={t0:G.t,x:p.x,y:p.y};
  const L=LEVELS[G.levelIdx];
  const swift=Math.max(0,L.par-G.moves)*15,cs=G.cogsGot*100,sc=250+swift+cs;
  G.lastClear={moves:G.moves,cogs:G.cogsGot,cogTotal:G.cogs.length,swift,score:sc};
  if(!G.run)G.run={score:0,moves:0,cogs:0,rows:[]};
  G.run.score+=sc;G.run.moves+=G.moves;G.run.cogs+=G.cogsGot;
  G.run.rows.push({name:L.name,moves:G.moves,cogs:G.cogsGot+"/"+G.cogs.length,score:sc});
  const s=loadSave();const prev=s.best&&s.best[G.levelIdx];
  G.lastClear.newBest=!prev||G.moves<prev;
  if(G.lastClear.newBest)persist({best:Object.assign({},s.best,{[G.levelIdx]:G.moves})});}
function showDeath(){if(G.ov==="death")return;G.ov="death";syncOverlays();
  $("deathReason").textContent=G.reason;
  $("dMoves").textContent=String(G.moves).padStart(2,"0");
  const s=loadSave(),b=s.best&&s.best[G.levelIdx];$("dBest").textContent=b?String(b):"—";}
function showClear(){G.ov="clear";syncOverlays();const c=G.lastClear,L=LEVELS[G.levelIdx];
  $("cName").textContent=L.name+" · LV "+(G.levelIdx+1);
  $("cMoves").textContent=c.moves+" / par "+L.par;
  $("cCogs").textContent=c.cogs+"/"+c.cogTotal+"  (+"+(c.cogs*100)+")";
  $("cSwift").textContent="+"+c.swift;
  $("cScore").textContent="+"+c.score;
  $("cNewBest").classList.toggle("hidden",!c.newBest);}
function showWin(){G.ov="win";syncOverlays();
  const sc=G.run.score;$("wRating").textContent=sc<1500?"apprentice winder":sc<2600?"journeyman of the steps":"MASTER OF THE CLOCKWORK";
  const tb=$("wTable");tb.innerHTML="";
  for(const r of G.run.rows){const k=document.createElement("span");k.className="k";k.textContent=r.name;
    const v=document.createElement("span");v.className="v";v.textContent=r.moves+" mv · "+r.cogs+"⚙";
    tb.appendChild(k);tb.appendChild(v);}
  $("wScore").textContent=sc;}

/* ================= particles / floaters ================= */
function burst(tx,ty,n,col,spd){for(let i=0;i<n;i++){const a=Math.random()*Math.PI*2,v=(.4+Math.random()*.8)*spd;
  G.particles.push({x:tx,y:ty,vx:Math.cos(a)*v,vy:Math.sin(a)*v-.6*spd,g:.0022,t0:G.t,life:500+Math.random()*400,size:.05+Math.random()*.09,col,shard:Math.random()<.4});}
  if(G.particles.length>380)G.particles.splice(0,G.particles.length-380);}
function addFloater(x,y,txt,col){G.floaters.push({x,y,txt,t0:G.t,col});}

/* ================= input ================= */
window.addEventListener("keydown",e=>{
  ensureAudio();
  const k=e.key;
  if(["ArrowLeft","ArrowRight","ArrowUp","ArrowDown"," "].includes(k))e.preventDefault();
  if(e.repeat)return;
  if(k==="m"||k==="M"){G.muted=!G.muted;if(master)master.gain.value=G.muted?0:0.5;persist({muted:G.muted});ticker(G.muted?"Sound off":"Sound on");return;}
  if(G.ov==="menu"){if(k==="Enter")startRun();return;}
  if(G.ov==="win"){if(k==="Enter")restartAll();return;}
  if(G.ov==="death"){if(k==="Enter"||k==="r"||k==="R")retryLevel();return;}
  if(G.ov==="clear"){if(k==="Enter")nextLevel();return;}
  if(G.ov==="pause"){if(k==="p"||k==="P"||k==="Escape"||k==="Enter")togglePause();else if(k==="r"||k==="R")retryLevel();return;}
  if(k==="p"||k==="P"||k==="Escape"){togglePause();return;}
  if(G.phase==="player"){
    if(k==="r"||k==="R"){retryLevel();return;}
    switch(k){
      case "ArrowLeft":case "a":case "A":stepMove(-1);break;
      case "ArrowRight":case "d":case "D":stepMove(1);break;
      case "ArrowUp":case "w":case "W":case " ":vault();break;
      case "ArrowDown":case "s":case "S":dropOrWait();break;}}
});
window.addEventListener("blur",()=>{if(!G.paused&&["player","pAnim","eAnim"].includes(G.phase))togglePause();});
function startRun(){ensureAudio();SFX.ui();G.run=null;buildLevel(0,false);}
function retryLevel(){SFX.ui();buildLevel(G.levelIdx,false);}
function nextLevel(){SFX.ui();if(G.levelIdx+1>=LEVELS.length){showWin();return;}buildLevel(G.levelIdx+1,false);}
function restartAll(){SFX.ui();G.run=null;buildLevel(0,false);}
function togglePause(){if(!["player","pAnim","eAnim"].includes(G.phase)&&G.ov!=="pause")return;
  G.paused=!G.paused;G.ov=G.paused?"pause":null;syncOverlays();SFX.ui();}
$("startBtn").onclick=e=>{e.target.blur();startRun();};
$("resumeBtn").onclick=e=>{e.target.blur();togglePause();};
$("retryBtn").onclick=e=>{e.target.blur();retryLevel();};
$("nextBtn").onclick=e=>{e.target.blur();nextLevel();};
$("againBtn").onclick=e=>{e.target.blur();restartAll();};

/* ================= canvas / resize ================= */
const cv=$("cv"),ctx=cv.getContext("2d");let dpr=1;
function resize(){dpr=Math.min(2,window.devicePixelRatio||1);G.vw=window.innerWidth;G.vh=window.innerHeight;
  cv.width=G.vw*dpr;cv.height=G.vh*dpr;
  G.T=clamp(Math.min(G.vw/21,G.vh/15),22,64);}
window.addEventListener("resize",resize);resize();

/* ================= update ================= */
function animPos(a,now){const pr=clamp((now-a.t0)/a.dur,0,1);return {pr,e:pr};}
function update(){
  // sequencer
  for(const it of G.seq)if(!it.done&&G.t>=it.at){it.done=true;it.fn();}
  // player anim finalize
  const p=G.player;
  if(p.anim&&G.act&&G.t-p.anim.t0>=p.anim.dur){const a=p.anim;p.anim=null;finalizeAction();}
  else if(p.anim&&!G.act&&G.t-p.anim.t0>=p.anim.dur){p.anim=null;if(G.pending){const f=G.pending.fn;G.pending=null;f();}}
  // squash decay
  p.sq*=Math.pow(.0025,1/60);
  // idle bob
  if(!p.dead&&!p.anim&&(G.phase==="player"||G.phase==="menu")){p.rx=p.x;p.ry=p.y+0.03*Math.sin(G.t*0.004);}
  else if(!p.dead&&!p.anim&&G.act===null){/* settle */}
  // enemies idle / anims
  for(const e of G.enemies){if(e.dead)continue;e.crouch*=Math.pow(.002,1/60);
    if(e.anim){const pr=clamp((G.t-e.anim.t0)/e.anim.dur,0,1);
      if(pr>=1)e.anim=null;else{
        e.rx=e.anim.fx+(e.x-e.anim.fx)*pr;
        if(e.anim.type==="hop"){e.ry=e.anim.fy-Math.sin(Math.PI*pr)*1.25;}
        else e.ry=e.anim.fy+(e.y-e.anim.fy)*pr;}}
    else{e.rx+=(e.x-e.rx)*0.35;e.ry+=(e.y-e.ry)*0.35;}}
  // bolts render
  for(const b of G.bolts){if(b.dead)continue;if(b.anim){const pr=clamp((G.t-b.anim.t0)/b.anim.dur,0,1);
      if(pr>=1)b.anim=null;else{b.rx=b.anim.fx+(b.x-b.anim.fx)*pr;b.ry=b.anim.fy+(b.y-b.anim.fy)*pr;}}
    else{b.rx+=(b.x-b.rx)*.4;b.ry+=.0;}}
  G.bolts=G.bolts.filter(b=>!b.dead);
  // particles / floaters
  for(const q of G.particles){q.x+=q.vx*0.16;q.y+=q.vy*0.16;q.vy+=q.g;}
  G.particles=G.particles.filter(q=>G.t-q.t0<q.life);
  G.floaters=G.floaters.filter(f=>G.t-f.t0<950);
  // shake
  G.shake=Math.max(0,G.shake-0.35);
  // camera
  const look=p.face*1.1;
  const tx=clamp(p.rx+look+0.5-G.viewW()/2,0,Math.max(0,G.W-G.viewW()));
  const ty=clamp(p.ry+0.5-G.viewH()/2,0,Math.max(0,G.H-G.viewH()));
  if(G.W<G.viewW())G.cam.x=(G.W-G.viewW())/2;else G.cam.x+=(tx-G.cam.x)*0.12;
  if(G.H<G.viewH())G.cam.y=(G.H-G.viewH())/2;else G.cam.y+=(ty-G.cam.y)*0.12;
  // delays
  if(G.phase==="dead"&&!G.ov&&G.t-G.deadAt>780)showDeath();
  if(G.phase==="clear"&&!G.ov&&G.t-G.clearAt>1050){if(G.levelIdx+1>=LEVELS.length)showWin();else showClear();}
}
G.viewW=()=>Math.ceil(G.vw/G.T);G.viewH=()=>Math.ceil(G.vh/G.T);

/* ================= render ================= */
function rr(x,y,w,h,r){ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(x+r,y);ctx.arcTo(x+w,y,x+w,y+h,r);ctx.arcTo(x+w,y+h,x,y+h,r);ctx.arcTo(x,y+h,x,y,r);ctx.arcTo(x,y,x+w,y,r);ctx.closePath();}
function gearPath(cx,cy,r,teeth,h,rot){ctx.beginPath();
  for(let i=0;i<teeth*2;i++){const a=rot+i*Math.PI/teeth,a2=a+Math.PI/teeth*.62,rad=i%2?r:r+h;
    const x=cx+Math.cos(a)*rad,y=cy+Math.sin(a)*rad;i?ctx.lineTo(x,y):ctx.moveTo(x,y);
    const x2=cx+Math.cos(a2)*(i%2?r:r+h),y2=cy+Math.sin(a2)*(i%2?r:r+h);ctx.lineTo(x2,y2);}
  ctx.closePath();}

function render(){
  ctx.setTransform(dpr,0,0,dpr,0,0);
  const W=G.vw,Hh=G.vh,T=G.T,L=LEVELS[G.levelIdx];
  // sky
  const sky=ctx.createLinearGradient(0,0,0,Hh);sky.addColorStop(0,L.sky[0]);sky.addColorStop(.55,L.sky[1]);sky.addColorStop(1,L.sky[2]);
  ctx.fillStyle=sky;ctx.fillRect(0,0,W,Hh);
  // gear-moon
  const mx=W*.72-G.cam.x*T*.04,my=Hh*.3-G.cam.y*T*.04,mr=Math.min(W,Hh)*.19;
  ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha=.16;ctx.fillStyle="#f0b45a";gearPath(mx,my,mr,12,mr*.22,G.t*0.0004);ctx.fill();
  ctx.globalCompositeOperation="destination-out";ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(mx,my,mr*.34,0,7);ctx.fill();ctx.restore();
  ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha=.1;ctx.strokeStyle="#ffd27a";ctx.lineWidth=2;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(mx,my,mr*1.12,0,7);ctx.stroke();ctx.restore();
  // skyline
  ctx.save();ctx.translate(-G.cam.x*T*.25,-G.cam.y*T*.12);ctx.fillStyle="rgba(16,22,46,.85)";
  const baseY=(G.H-3.4)*T;
  for(const b of G.skyline){const bx=b.x*T,bw=b.w*T,bh=b.h*T;
    ctx.fillRect(bx,baseY-bh,bw,bh+T*8);
    if(b.cog){gearPath(bx+bw/2,baseY-bh-bh*.4,bh*.22,7,G.t*.0003+b.x);ctx.fill();}}
  ctx.restore();
  // mist band
  const mist=ctx.createLinearGradient(0,Hh*.62,0,Hh*.95);mist.addColorStop(0,"rgba(240,180,90,0)");mist.addColorStop(.6,"rgba(240,170,90,.07)");mist.addColorStop(1,"rgba(240,180,90,0)");
  ctx.fillStyle=mist;ctx.fillRect(0,Hh*.6,W,Hh*.4);
  // world
  const shx=(Math.random()*2-1)*G.shake,shy=(Math.random()*2-1)*G.shake;
  ctx.save();ctx.translate(-Math.round(G.cam.x*T)+shx,-Math.round(G.cam.y*T)+shy);
  const x0=Math.max(0,Math.floor(G.cam.x)-1),x1=Math.min(G.W,x0+G.viewW()+2);
  const y0=Math.max(0,Math.floor(G.cam.y)-1),y1=Math.min(G.H,y0+G.viewH()+2);
  for(let y=y0;y<y1;y++)for(let x=x0;x<x1;x++){const t=G.grid[y][x];if(!t)continue;const px=x*T,py=y*T;
    if(t===T_SOLID){ctx.fillStyle="#33406b";ctx.fillRect(px,py,T,T);
      if(tileAt(x,y-1)!==T_SOLID&&tileAt(x,y-1)!==T_CRUMB){ctx.fillStyle="#4c5b93";ctx.fillRect(px,py,T,T*.2);
        ctx.fillStyle="rgba(255,255,255,.14)";ctx.fillRect(px,py,T,2);}
      ctx.fillStyle="rgba(0,0,0,.22)";ctx.fillRect(px,py+T-3,T,3);
      const h=hash2(x,y);if(h%3===0){ctx.fillStyle="#242e52";ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(px+T*.25,py+T*.6,2.2,0,7);ctx.arc(px+T*.75,py+T*.4,2.2,0,7);ctx.fill();}}
    else if(t===T_CRUMB){const marked=G.marks.has(x+","+y);const ox=marked?Math.sin(G.t*.06+x)*1.6:0;
      ctx.save();ctx.translate(ox,0);
      ctx.fillStyle="#5d4a33";ctx.fillRect(px,py,T,T);ctx.fillStyle="#7c6244";ctx.fillRect(px,py,T,T*.2);
      ctx.strokeStyle="rgba(20,14,8,.7)";ctx.lineWidth=1.5;const h=hash2(x,y);
      ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(px+T*(.2+h%3*.1),py+2);ctx.lineTo(px+T*.5,py+T*.55);ctx.lineTo(px+T*(.6-h%4*.08),py+T-2);ctx.stroke();
      if(marked){ctx.fillStyle="rgba(255,90,60,"+(0.15+0.15*Math.sin(G.t*.02))+")";ctx.fillRect(px,py,T,T);}
      ctx.restore();}
    else if(t===T_PLATE){ctx.fillStyle="#d9a13f";ctx.fillRect(px,py,T,T*.24);
      ctx.fillStyle="#f6cd7e";ctx.fillRect(px,py,T,2.5);ctx.fillStyle="#8a5a1c";ctx.fillRect(px,py+T*.24-3,T,3);
      ctx.fillStyle="#6e4712";ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(px+5,py+T*.12,2,0,7);ctx.arc(px+T-5,py+T*.12,2,0,7);ctx.fill();}
    else if(t===T_SPIKE){const n=3,w=T/n;ctx.fillStyle="#8fa0c4";
      for(let i=0;i<n;i++){ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(px+i*w,py+T);ctx.lineTo(px+i*w+w*.5,py+T*.22);ctx.lineTo(px+(i+1)*w,py+T);ctx.closePath();ctx.fill();}
      ctx.fillStyle="#ff6b5c";for(let i=0;i<n;i++){ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(px+i*w+w*.5,py+T*.3,w*.12,0,7);ctx.fill();}
      ctx.fillStyle="#2a3358";ctx.fillRect(px,py+T-4,T,4);}
    else if(t===T_DOOR){const p=G.player,near=Math.abs(p.x-x)+Math.abs(p.y-y)<5;
      const pulse=.5+.5*Math.sin(G.t*.004);
      ctx.fillStyle="#1a2140";ctx.fillRect(px+T*.08,py+T*.1,T*.84,T*.9);
      const g=ctx.createLinearGradient(0,py,0,py+T);g.addColorStop(0,"rgba(255,210,122,"+(near?.85:.5)+")");g.addColorStop(1,"rgba(255,150,60,.15)");
      ctx.fillStyle=g;ctx.fillRect(px+T*.2,py+T*.16,T*.6,T*.8);
      ctx.strokeStyle="#d98a2b";ctx.lineWidth=3;ctx.strokeRect(px+T*.14,py+T*.06,T*.72,T*.94);
      ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha=.5+.5*pulse;ctx.fillStyle="#ffd27a";gearPath(px+T*.5,py-T*.14,T*.13,6,T*.05,G.t*.002);ctx.fill();ctx.restore();}}
  // cogs
  for(const c of G.cogs){if(c.taken)continue;const bob=Math.sin(G.t*.003+c.x)*.06;
    ctx.save();ctx.translate((c.x+.5)*T,(c.y+.5+bob)*T);
    ctx.fillStyle="#f0b45a";gearPath(0,0,T*.24,8,T*.09,G.t*.0012+c.x);ctx.fill();
    ctx.fillStyle="#1a2140";ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(0,0,T*.09,0,7);ctx.fill();
    if((G.t/300|0)%7===(c.x%7)){ctx.fillStyle="rgba(255,255,255,.8)";ctx.fillRect(-T*.1,-T*.2,3,3);}
    ctx.restore();}
  // enemies
  const foeGlow=G.phase==="eAnim";
  for(const e of G.enemies){if(e.dead)continue;const ex=e.rx*T,ey=(e.ry+1)*T;
    ctx.save();if(foeGlow){ctx.shadowColor="rgba(255,140,80,.9)";ctx.shadowBlur=12;}
    if(e.type==="crawl"){const al=e.asleep?.5:1;ctx.globalAlpha=al;
      const leg=Math.sin(G.t*.012+e.x*3)* (e.asleep?0:1);
      ctx.strokeStyle="#7e2a2a";ctx.lineWidth=2.5;
      for(let i=0;i<3;i++){const lx=ex+T*(.2+i*.3),lo=leg*(i%2?1:-1)*T*.1;
        ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(lx,ey-T*.18);ctx.lineTo(lx-lo,ey);ctx.stroke();}
      ctx.fillStyle="#ff6b6b";rr(ex+T*.12,ey-T*.5,T*.76,T*.42,T*.09);ctx.fill();
      ctx.strokeStyle="#3a0d0d";ctx.lineWidth=2;rr(ex+T*.12,ey-T*.5,T*.76,T*.42,T*.09);ctx.stroke();
      const p=G.player,dx=Math.sign(p.rx-e.rx)||e.face;
      ctx.fillStyle="#2a0703";ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(ex+T*(.5+dx*.16),ey-T*.34,T*.08,0,7);ctx.fill();
      ctx.fillStyle="#ffd27a";ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(ex+T*(.5+dx*.16)+dx*1.5,ey-T*.35,T*.03,0,7);ctx.fill();}
    else if(e.type==="hop"){const cr=e.crouch,sy=1-cr*.45;
      ctx.strokeStyle="#2c8a6b";ctx.lineWidth=2.5;
      for(let i=0;i<3;i++){ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(ex+T*.5,ey-T*.07-i*3,T*(.2-i*.03),Math.PI,0);ctx.stroke();}
      ctx.save();ctx.translate(ex+T*.5,ey-T*.16);ctx.scale(1+cr*.3,sy);
      ctx.fillStyle="#57d9a3";ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(0,-T*.2,T*.26,0,7);ctx.fill();
      ctx.strokeStyle="#124834";ctx.lineWidth=2;ctx.stroke();
      const p=G.player,dx=Math.sign(p.rx-e.rx)||e.face;
      ctx.fillStyle="#0c2b1f";ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(-T*.09+dx*2,-T*.24,T*.055,0,7);ctx.arc(T*.09+dx*2,-T*.24,T*.055,0,7);ctx.fill();
      ctx.restore();}
    else{const willFire=(G.foeCount+1)%3===0&&G.phase!=="dead";
      ctx.fillStyle="#8fa3c8";rr(ex+T*.12,ey-T*.6,T*.76,T*.5,T*.06);ctx.fill();
      ctx.strokeStyle="#242e52";ctx.lineWidth=2.5;rr(ex+T*.12,ey-T*.6,T*.76,T*.5,T*.06);ctx.stroke();
      const bx=e.face>0?ex+T*.8:ex-T*.34;ctx.fillStyle="#6d80ab";ctx.fillRect(bx,ey-T*.44,T*.34,T*.18);
      const glow=willFire?.5+.5*Math.sin(G.t*.02):.25;
      ctx.save();if(willFire){ctx.shadowColor="rgba(255,90,70,.9)";ctx.shadowBlur=14;}
      ctx.fillStyle="rgba(255,80,60,"+(0.5+glow*.5)+")";ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(ex+T*.5,ey-T*.35,T*(.09+glow*.05),0,7);ctx.fill();ctx.restore();
      ctx.fillStyle="#4d5f92";ctx.fillRect(ex+T*.12,ey-T*.12,T*.76,T*.12);}
    ctx.restore();}
  // bolts
  for(const b of G.bolts){const bx=b.rx*T+T*.5,by=b.ry*T+T*.5+Math.sin(G.t*.02+b.x)*T*.05;
    for(let i=1;i<=3;i++){ctx.fillStyle="rgba(255,210,122,"+(0.3-i*.08)+")";ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(bx-b.dir*i*T*.22,by,T*.1*(1-i*.2),0,7);ctx.fill();}
    ctx.save();ctx.shadowColor="rgba(255,210,122,.9)";ctx.shadowBlur=14;
    ctx.fillStyle="#ffd27a";ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(bx,by,T*.16,0,7);ctx.fill();
    ctx.fillStyle="#fff3d6";ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(bx,by,T*.07,0,7);ctx.fill();ctx.restore();}
  // player
  const p=G.player;
  if(!p.dead)drawPlayer(p);
  // particles
  for(const q of G.particles){const a=1-(G.t-q.t0)/q.life;if(a<=0)continue;
    ctx.globalAlpha=a;ctx.fillStyle=q.col;
    const s=q.size*T*(0.5+a*.7);
    if(q.shard)ctx.fillRect(q.x*T-s/2,q.y*T-s/2,s,s*.7);else{ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(q.x*T,q.y*T,s/2,0,7);ctx.fill();}}
  ctx.globalAlpha=1;
  // floaters
  for(const f of G.floaters){const pr=(G.t-f.t0)/950;ctx.globalAlpha=1-pr;
    ctx.font="700 "+Math.round(T*.3)+"px "+getComputedStyle(document.body).fontFamily;
    ctx.fillStyle=f.col;ctx.textAlign="center";ctx.fillText(f.txt,f.x*T,(f.y-pr*.8)*T);}
  ctx.globalAlpha=1;
  ctx.restore();
  // embers (screen space)
  drawEmbers(W,Hh);
  // foe tint
  if(G.phase==="eAnim"){ctx.fillStyle="rgba(255,70,60,"+(0.04+0.03*Math.sin(G.t*.012))+")";ctx.fillRect(0,0,W,Hh);}
  // vignette
  const vg=ctx.createRadialGradient(W/2,Hh/2,Math.min(W,Hh)*.42,W/2,Hh/2,Math.max(W,Hh)*.75);
  vg.addColorStop(0,"rgba(0,0,0,0)");vg.addColorStop(1,"rgba(5,8,18,.55)");ctx.fillStyle=vg;ctx.fillRect(0,0,W,Hh);
  // iris wipe on clear
  if(G.iris&&G.phase==="clear"){const pr=clamp((G.t-G.iris.t0)/750,0,1),e=pr*pr*(3-2*pr);
    const dx=(G.iris.x+.5-G.cam.x)*T,dy=(G.iris.y+.5-G.cam.y)*T,r=e*Math.hypot(W,Hh)*1.1;
    ctx.fillStyle="#0a0e1c";ctx.beginPath();ctx.rect(0,0,W,Hh);ctx.arc(dx,dy,Math.max(1,r),0,7,true);ctx.fill("evenodd");}}

function drawPlayer(p){const T=G.T;let rx=p.rx,ry=p.ry,sx=1,sy=1;
  if(p.anim){const a=p.anim,pr=clamp((G.t-a.t0)/a.dur,0,1);
    if(a.type==="step"){rx=a.fx+(p.x-a.fx)*pr;ry=a.fy;}
    else if(a.type==="vault"){rx=a.fx+(p.x-a.fx)*pr;ry=a.fy+(p.y-a.fy)*pr-Math.sin(Math.PI*pr)*.35*a.h;sy=1+.3*Math.sin(Math.PI*pr);sx=1-.2*Math.sin(Math.PI*pr);}
    else if(a.type==="drop"){rx=p.x;ry=a.fy+(a.toY!==undefined?a.toY:p.y-a.fy)*pr;}
    else if(a.type==="fall"){const e=pr*pr;ry=a.fromY+(a.toY-a.fromY)*e;sy=1+.18*Math.sin(Math.PI*pr);sx=.92;}
    else if(a.type==="bump"){rx=p.x+Math.sin(pr*Math.PI)*.14*a.dir;}}
  const x=(rx+.5)*T,feet=(ry+1)*T;
  ctx.save();ctx.translate(x,feet);ctx.scale(sx*(1+p.sq*.3),sy*(1-p.sq*.32));
  const bw=T*.62,bh=T*.66;
  ctx.fillStyle="#ffd27a";rr(-bw/2,-bh,bw,bh,T*.14);ctx.fill();
  ctx.strokeStyle="#241503";ctx.lineWidth=2.5;rr(-bw/2,-bh,bw,bh,T*.14);ctx.stroke();
  const look=p.anim&&p.anim.type==="fall"?0:p.face;
  ctx.fillStyle="#2a2140";rr(-bw*.36+look*bw*.1,-bh+bh*.18,bw*.72,bh*.34,T*.08);ctx.fill();
  const ey=-bh+bh*.35,ex=look*bw*.14;
  ctx.fillStyle="#9ff0ff";ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(ex,ey,bw*.11,0,7);ctx.fill();
  ctx.fillStyle="#0c2b3a";ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(ex+look*1.5,ey,bw*.05,0,7);ctx.fill();
  ctx.strokeStyle="#d98a2b";ctx.lineWidth=2;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(0,-bh);ctx.lineTo(0,-bh-T*.14);ctx.stroke();
  ctx.fillStyle="#ff6b5c";ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(Math.sin(G.t*.006)*2,-bh-T*.16,T*.05,0,7);ctx.fill();
  ctx.restore();}

let embers=[];
function drawEmbers(W,Hh){if(embers.length<46&&Math.random()<.3)embers.push({x:Math.random()*W,y:Hh+10,v:.25+Math.random()*.5,ph:Math.random()*7,a:.15+Math.random()*.3});
  ctx.save();for(const e of embers){e.y-=e.v;e.x+=Math.sin(G.t*.001+e.ph)*.3;
    if(e.y<-10){e.y=Hh+10;e.x=Math.random()*W;}
    ctx.globalAlpha=e.a*(0.6+0.4*Math.sin(G.t*.003+e.ph));ctx.fillStyle="#f0b45a";
    ctx.fillRect(e.x,e.y,2,2);}ctx.restore();}

/* ================= main loop ================= */
let lastTs=0;
function frame(ts){requestAnimationFrame(frame);const dt=Math.min(50,ts-(lastTs||ts));lastTs=ts;
  if(!G.paused)G.t+=dt;
  update();render();}
buildLevel(0,true);
requestAnimationFrame(frame);
</script>
</body>
</html>
```
